# Remote read and write parity

One export represents one fleet; each case and repetition is independent, never pooled. Latency is successful API calls, nearest-rank percentiles as supplied by analyze.py; whole-case rate includes time outside API calls. Cold qualification uses device reads per successful call >= 0.9 and the receipt verdict; this proxy is not proof of physical access. No significance or general ranking is implied.

## Native write parity

Caption: Unmatched dimensions: durable mechanics and sync scope (Native page cache is not durable; Native barrier fsyncs registered files and directory per call, grouped barrier shares calls; owner synchronous uses covering fdatasync on an owner file); network in the data path (Native in-process, owner RPC); index (Native Db index versus coordinator index and owner history); routing (Native key hash versus owner operation ID); instances and SSD count (Native one process and one SSD versus coordinator plus one owner and one owner SSD). Floors are separate populations, not components.
